# The names of classes in Qt's source that cutienator does not cover
# yet, each with the header file that defines it, as a path in Qt's
# source (qt-everywhere-src-6.12.0). Covered are the classes of
# tmp/scratch/next/picked9.txt at the time of writing.
#
# Found in the text of every header file under a 'src' folder of each
# module, leaving out '3rdparty', 'tests', 'examples', 'plugins',
# 'tools', 'doc' and 'snippets': a line that begins with 'class' or
# 'struct' and defines a class whose name begins with 'Q'. A class
# written inside another class is not in it. A name defined in more
# than one header file stands once for each.
#
# 'HeaderReader' looks a header file up here by its name when the
# compiler cannot find it.
